# Roubit : Fun Routine Planner (App Store ID 1527382961) — Full Review Analysis

**Corpus:** all **500** text reviews in `App Store Reviews/83. Roubit  - Fun Routine Planner - Bunny Diary & Tasks/reviews.jsonl`
**Window:** 2020-11-14 → 2026-09-01 (2,118 days; 346 of the 500 reviews fall on or after 2024-01-01) · **Storefronts:** 40 · **Languages:** 14 · **Corpus mean rating: 4.156★**
**Developer:** Roubit Inc. ((주)루빗, Korea; bundle `com.roubit`) · **Released:** 2020-11-05 · **Current version:** 6.5.9 (2026-09-08) · **Store name in Korea:** 루빗 - 귀여운 습관 할일 투두 계획 자기계발 루틴
**Method:** every review read individually in its original language; 112 theme codes across 13 families (108 hand-assigned, 4 mechanical) and 22 composite unions; 2,007 code assignments; 85 integrity checks; 24 absence tests; external evidence captured 13 September 2026.

---

## How to read this

Every quantified claim carries a **count, a percentage, the denominator, the market scope, the time window, a signal label and exact review IDs**. Review IDs are `review_id` values from `reviews.jsonl`; the bracketed `#n` is the working index (date order) used by Part 10, so any number resolves to specific reviews and any review resolves to the findings it supports. §9.J lists every review ID behind every code.

Theme counts are **non-exclusive** — a five-star review that praises the bunny and reports a blank diary screen is counted in both. Composite unions (`U:` prefix) count each review **once**. Unless a sentence names a narrower scope, the denominator is **500 reviews, all 40 storefronts, 2020-11-14 → 2026-09-01**. Claims written as `` `CODE` = n (x%) `` always use that denominator; segment rates are written "n of N".

### ⚠️ Nine warnings before any number here is used

1. **These reviewers are not a negatively selected minority.** The same 40 storefronts carry **3,320 public ratings** averaging **4.658★**; this corpus averages **4.156★**, a gap of **+0.502★**. The gap is small in Korea (public 4.71★ vs corpus 4.37★) and the US (4.72★ vs 4.20★) and large in Japan (4.48★ vs 3.36★). Most text reviews here are written by people who like the app.
2. **Only three storefronts reach the 50-review threshold:** South Korea (KR 246), the United States (US 61) and Japan (JP 56) — together 363 of 500 (72.60%). The other 37 storefronts hold 137 reviews, and 17 storefronts have exactly one review. Nothing said about them is more than limited evidence (§6.6).
3. **Half the corpus is Korean.** 251 Korean reviews (50.20%), 121 English, 55 Japanese, and 11 further languages, all read in the original. Translations are the analyst's own and are labelled.
4. **Reviews carry no version field**, and the store shows dated release notes only from 2025-09-16. Every link between a release and what reviewers say is **timing, not proven cause**.
5. **The last ten months are two bug bursts.** Era E4 (2025-11-06 → 2026-09-01) holds 117 reviews; 40 report the Diary or Settings tab opening a blank screen, in two waves (§0.2, §7). Many of these are support tickets written as reviews — `SUPPORT_QUESTION` = 43 (8.60%) — and 31 of the 33 rating-versus-text contradictions are four- or five-star bug reports (§4.7).
6. **145 reviews (29.00%) have under 60 characters of body text** and only 31 reviews (6.20%) carry helpful votes, 17 of the 31 of them in Japan. §9.D re-runs the headline shares without the short reviews, without Korea and without E4.
7. **Purchase evidence is what reviewers say.** `PAID_EXPLICIT` = 27 (5.40%) say they pay or paid. That is not a conversion rate.
8. **Some reviews were prompted.** Four reviews from January–March 2022 say they were written during a developer survey or for an in-app "review-writing mission", and three post invite codes for in-app rewards (§9.H). They are kept at the star the reviewer gave.
9. **Two reviews describe suicidal thoughts or a personal crisis** that the app helped with (§0.3, §3.11). They are safety-relevant evidence, not only praise.

---

# PART 0 — READ THIS FIRST

## 0.1 The bunny, the room and the letters are what people are paying attention to

**`U:praise_any` = 369 (73.80%, CI 69.8–77.5%, mean 4.60★, High-priority signal)**; **`U:praise_specific` = 284 (56.80%)** name a quality.

| Code / union | n | % of 500 | What it records |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| `U:character_game` | 192 | 38.40% | the bunny/art is cute, or the carrot → coin → room loop motivates |
| `PRAISE_CUTE` | 157 | 31.40% | bunny, art, colours called cute or pretty |
| `OUT_HABIT_BUILT` | 97 | 19.40% | the app helped build habits, consistency or productivity |
| `PRAISE_GAMIFICATION` | 76 | 15.20% | carrots, coins, room decorating, levels, gacha motivate |
| `U:letter_ai_praise` | 74 | 14.80% | the AI support letter replying to a diary entry, or Dr. Eddy |
| `OUT_EMOTIONAL_SUPPORT` | 64 | 12.80% | comfort, mood, stress, depression, loneliness |

**The game loop gets people to open the app, and opening it gets the routine done.** `11575242405` (#219, 2024-08-05, kr, 5★) — "방꾸미려고 앱 들어가서 자동으로 할일까지 다 하는느낌" *[I go in to decorate the room and end up doing all my tasks automatically]*. `8290633106` (#35, 2022-01-28, kr, 5★) installed five highly rated apps: "마지막에 살아남은 단 하나의 앱은 루빗입니다" *[the only one that survived was Roubit]*. `13956187658` (#443, 2026-04-14, jp, 3★), while uninstalling, still calls the idea "「習慣化アプリ×ガチャゲーム」とした発想は今でも面白く、秀逸なものだと思っている" *[the idea of 'habit app × gacha game' is still interesting and excellent]*.

**The support letter is the feature people single out most since it launched.** It first appears on 2023-04-27 (`9866716493`, #107, kr) — "요번에 업데이트된 편지 발송 넘 좋은 것 같아요" *[the letter sending in this update is great]*. In era E3 (2023-04-27 → 2025-11-05) `PRAISE_SUPPORT_LETTER` is 60 of 277 (21.7%). `12161555324` (#281, 2025-01-09, br, 5★) — "As respostas do diário são melhores do que quando eu converso com amigos meus." *[the diary replies are better than talking with my friends]*.

## 0.2 In two waves, the Diary and Settings tabs stopped opening

- **`BUG_DIARY_SETTINGS_BLANK` = 40 (8.00%, CI 5.9–10.7%, High-priority signal)** — Diary and/or Settings opens a white screen or does not respond. It is first on 2025-11-06 and last on 2026-06-09; **none before**.
- **Wave 1:** 14 of the 15 reviews posted 2025-11-06 → 2025-11-10 report it (KR 6, JP 8). **Wave 2:** 26 of the 37 reviews posted 2026-05-16 → 2026-06-09 report it, and 36 of the 37 report some defect.
- **Settings holds the support form and account deletion, so the bug also cut off the way to report it.** `SUPPORT_UNREACHABLE_INAPP` = 6 (1.20%). `13372186913` (#395, 2025-11-08, jp, 4★) — "設定にもいけないから不具合報告が出来なくてやむなくこちらに書かせて頂きました" *[I can't reach Settings to report the bug, so I had no choice but to write here]*. `14123175914` (#477, 2026-05-30, jp, 1★) — "設定画面に入れないので何もできない" *[I can't get into Settings, so I can't do anything]* (about deleting the account).
- **The diary holds years of personal writing.** `DATA_LOSS` = 11 (2.20%). `13370906696` (#391, 2025-11-08, kr, 5★) — "제 2년의 추억이 모두 들어가있는 앱 입니다" *[this app holds two years of my memories]*. `14093228593` (#471, 2026-05-22, kr, 3★) — "적어도 1년동안 쓴 일기라도 보고싶은데 그것도 안되고" *[I'd at least like to see the diary I wrote for a year, but I can't]*.
- **Loyal users filed these, often with five stars.** 19 of the 40 blank-screen reports are four- or five-star. `14074890592` (#458, 2026-05-17, kr, 5★), level 70 after 18 months — "70렙찍고 이렇게 됬는데 제발 제 최애 인생 루틴앱인데 고쳐주시면 감사하겠습니다" *[I hit level 70 and now this; please fix my favourite routine app]*.

The developer's 2026-06-09 release note (6.4.52) says "If the diary list cannot be loaded, a helpful message is shown instead of a blank screen" [external, §9.E]. Reports of the blank Diary tab continue to 2026-06-09 and stop after it; with only 11 reviews after that date, **the stop is weak evidence of a fix**.

## 0.3 For a meaningful minority, this is a mental-health tool

- **`U:mental_health_any` = 68 (13.60%, CI 10.9–16.9%, mean 4.84★)**; `USE_MENTAL_HEALTH` = 16 (3.20%) name depression, anxiety, ADHD, autism, a crisis or suicidal thoughts.
- `10866655553` (#166, 2024-01-26, kr, 5★) — "몇년간 우울증에 허덕이는 저를 조금이라도 움직일수 있게 해주는 앱이에요" *[an app that gets me, who has struggled with depression for years, to move even a little]*.
- `13303553647` (#383, 2025-10-23, gb, 5★) — "I am autistic and find it very hard to manage routines".
- **Two reviews are safety-relevant.** `12738770364` (#347, 2025-06-05, kr, 5★) — "제가 죽고싶었을때가 있는데 이거 덕분에 지금 제가 여기있었어요" *[there was a time I wanted to die; thanks to this I'm here now]*. `13873857942` (#436, 2026-03-22, cl, 5★) — "No es una exageración decir que esta aplicación me salvó la vida." *[it is no exaggeration to say this app saved my life]*. One reviewer notes the letters point to professional help: `13429711572` (#405, br) — "sempre indicando a procura por um profissional" *[always recommending seeking a professional]*.

**What follows from this:** the letter feature is receiving crisis-level disclosures. How it handles them is a product-safety question, not only a delight feature (§8.3).

## 0.4 Many people never got in

- **`U:cant_use_app` = 88 (17.60%, CI 14.5–21.2%, mean 3.23★)** — blank Diary/Settings, `BUG_LAUNCH_LOAD` = 26 (5.20%), `BUG_LOGIN_SIGNUP` = 16 (3.20%), `BUG_CRASH` = 11 (2.20%), `BUG_UPDATE_LOOP` = 7 (1.40%).
- **In the US the problem is getting in at all:** `BUG_LAUNCH_LOAD` is 7 of 61 (11.5%) US reviews, against 4.3% elsewhere. `14320708889` (#495, 2026-07-19, us, 1★) — "i have never been able to use it because it does not load". `13156744094` (#376, 2025-09-19, ae, 1★) — "it just infinitely loads. Unusable."
- **`U:defect_any` = 180 (36.00%)**. Across all ratings, 46 of the 62 one- and two-star reviews describe a defect or being blocked from the app.

## 0.5 Monetisation friction is mild; price and the lack of a one-time option are the Korean objections

- **`U:paywall_friction` = 61 (12.20%, mean 3.85★)** — spread thinly: `FREE_LIMIT_ROUTINES` = 12 (2.40%), `ADS_IN_APP` = 12 (2.40%), `PRICE_TOO_HIGH` = 10 (2.00%), `PAYWALL_LETTERS_AI` = 8 (1.60%), `PRICE_OPAQUE` = 8 (1.60%), `WANT_ONE_TIME_PURCHASE` = 6 (1.20%).
- **The free tier is praised more than it is attacked.** `PRAISE_FREE_GENEROUS` = 14 (2.80%). `14479724385` (#499, 2026-08-27, gb, 5★) — "literally gives you supportive letters every day FOR FREE".
- **Korea:** price and subscription format. `11959502740` (#257, 2024-11-17, kr, 5★) — "동물의숲이 평생에 5만원인데 루틴 앱이 1년에 5만5천원" *[Animal Crossing is ₩50,000 for life; a routine app is ₩55,000 a year]* … "결제를 못하겠어서 속상한 앱은 처음이에요" *[this is the first app where I'm upset that I can't bring myself to pay]*. `WOULD_PAY_INTENT` is 11 of 246 (4.5%) in Korea and 1 of 254 elsewhere.
- **Paying users:** `PAID_EXPLICIT` = 27 (5.40%); 12 of 27 (44.4%) are satisfied, and 14 of 27 (51.9%) report a defect (§5).

## 0.6 Ads are moving into the core loop

- **`U:ads_any` = 16 (3.20%)** — `ADS_IN_APP` = 12, `ADS_FOR_PAST_CHECK` = 4 (0.80%): checking off yesterday's routine requires watching an ad.
- `8330938138` (#38, 2022-02-08, kr, 5★) — "전 날 놓친 기록 체크하려면 기록 하나 하나 광고를 봐야 체크할 수 있게 해놓은 건 굉장히 많이 불편하네요" *[making me watch an ad for each record just to check off the previous day is very inconvenient]*.
- **In 2026, ads cost the app long-time users.** `13877341723` (#437, 2026-03-23, jp, 1★) — "アプリを開く時も日記を書く時も広告が流れるようになりました" *[ads now play when opening the app and when writing the diary]*. `14149256600` (#484, 2026-06-06, au, 1★), a user of several years — "갑자기 광고가 생기더니 광고 다 봐도 루빗에 아예 접속이 안되네요" *[ads suddenly appeared, and even after watching them I can't get into Roubit at all]*. `13956187658` (#443, jp, 3★) uninstalled after a month in which the past-day ad would not load, so past tasks could not be completed.
- Release notes: 2026-04-29 "A new option to watch ads and earn additional rewards"; 2026-06-09 "Premium users can now receive rewards without waiting for ads" [external].

## 0.7 The app spread internationally before it spoke other languages

- **English speakers arrived in 2022 through Twitter to a Korean-only app.** `LANG_REQUEST` = 11 (2.20%), first on 2022-08-20; in era E2 (2022-08-01 → 2023-04-26) it is 7 of 38 (18.4%) reviews. `9049739687` (#75, 2022-09-04, ph, 4★) — "i downloaded it and tried my best to translate each part but its tiring". `9047952818` (#74, id) — "in developer the language said it was on korean and english but inside the app you can only use korean".
- **Korean text still appears in 2026.** `LOCALIZATION_INCOMPLETE` = 5 (1.00%): `14090994337` (#470, 2026-05-21, jp) — "ハングルで読めない。" *[it's in Hangul, I can't read it]* (the gacha); `14017493057` (#447, 2026-05-01, mx) — "me aparecen letras coreanas" *[Korean letters appear]* (the store tab).
- Requests for Chinese (`11397949222`, `13458634572`, `13868299608`) and Vietnamese (`11042639191`) remain open; the listing supports English and Korean only [external].

## 0.8 Reviews are being used as a support desk

- `SUPPORT_QUESTION` = 43 (8.60%) ask the developer something; `META_RATING_TEXT_MISMATCH` = 33 (6.60%) — **31 of the 33 are four- or five-star reviews that contain only a complaint or question**, and 27 of the 31 report a defect.
- `U:support_negative` = 15 (3.00%, mean 2.53★): unanswered email (`14138599777` "担当者の方へメールも2度しましたが、返信もなく" *[I emailed the person in charge twice with no reply]*), unread KakaoTalk messages (`14142547272`), a promised same-day fix followed by three days of silence (`13852438902`).
- **Support that worked changed the rating.** `13812322981` (#426, 2026-03-04, tr) moved to five stars — "The support helped me fix the ad issue by using the “Restore Subscription” button." `13303553647` (#383) — "the only issue / bug I've had was sorted within 24 hours".

## 0.9 What this corpus shows is *absent*

Tested against all 500 reviews with multilingual patterns; every hit was read (§9.F). **No Family Sharing mention, no Android comparison, no data-export or backup request, no battery complaint (one overheating report, `11213578473`), and one tracking objection (`13473580389`).** Apple Watch or iPad support is requested only from Korea (`REQ_IPAD_WATCH_MAC` = 15, all KR). Refunds are mentioned twice.

# PART 1 — SCOPE AND METHOD

## 1.1 Files used

| Path | Role |
|---|---|
| `App Store Reviews/83. …/reviews.jsonl` | **The corpus.** 500 records, one JSON object per line. |
| `App Store Reviews/83. …/by_country/*.jsonl` | 40 per-storefront files, 500 records total. |
| `App Store Reviews/83. …/manifest.json` | Extraction metadata (2026-09-08): 500 reviews, 40 storefronts, rating distribution 301/83/54/17/45, mean 4.156. |
| `App Store Reviews/83. …/_state.json` | Crawl state for **90 storefronts**: 40 with reviews, 50 swept and empty, 0 incomplete. |

The source folder is read-only; nothing was written to it. All working artefacts are in `Temp/` (`83-*`).

## 1.2 Schema actually present

13 fields, populated on all 500 records, with no nulls and no empty strings:

| Field | Notes |
|---|---|
| `review_id` | Primary key. **500 unique, no duplicates.** 11 digits (385) or 10 digits (115). |
| `app_id` / `app_name` | 1527382961 / `Roubit : Fun Routine Planner` throughout. |
| `country` / `country_name` | 40 storefronts. **Storefront, not language** (§6.7). |
| `rating` | 1–5, all five values present. |
| `title` | "너무 좋아요" 6 times; "좋아요", "루빗" and "Roubit" 5 each. No title+body pair repeats. |
| `body` | All 500 distinct; median body length 104.5 characters. |
| `author` | All 500 distinct. **No repeat authors.** |
| `date` | ISO-8601 UTC. Up to 8 reviews on one calendar day (§9.H). |
| `vote_count` / `vote_sum` | 31 reviews carry helpful votes; maximum 5. |
| `is_edited` | 22 reviews are edited. |

**There is no `version` field.**

## 1.3 Coverage and reconciliation

Run by `83-reconcile.py`; output in `Temp/83-reconcile-out.txt`. Every check is clean.

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| Records read from `reviews.jsonl` | **500 — 100% of the corpus** |
| Unique `review_id` | 500 (**0 duplicates**) |
| Records across the 40 `by_country` files | 500; ID set identical to the main file |
| Country records not identical, field by field, to the main-file record | **0** |
| Country field ≠ file name | **0** |
| `manifest.json` total / per-country counts / storefront count / rating distribution | **all match exactly** |
| Mean rating recomputed vs manifest | 4.156 vs 4.156 ✓ |
| `_state.json` collected counts vs main file | **0 differences**; 90 storefronts swept, 0 incomplete |
| Exact title+body duplicates / exact body duplicates | **0 / 0** |
| Repeat author names | **0** |

**No deduplication was necessary or performed.**

## 1.4 Processing method

1. `83-dump.py` — parse all 500 records, HTML-unescape, sort by `(date, review_id)`, emit `83-all-reviews.txt` (every review in full) and `83-index.tsv`.
2. **Every review read individually, in full, in its original language, in date order.** No sampling, no keyword filtering, no clustering. Korean (251), English (121), Japanese (55), Portuguese (26), Spanish (22), Turkish, French and Chinese (5 each), German (3), Russian, Hungarian, Vietnamese, Indonesian and Dutch (1 each), plus two emoji-only reviews.
3. `83-themes.py` — a 112-code, 13-family taxonomy built **from** the corpus after reading it, with a definition and direction for every code, plus 22 unions. **Every code is used.**
4. `83-cls/001-100.txt` … `401-500.txt` — the hand classification, five files: one line per review with the working index, the `review_id`, its codes, its language and a note quoting the exact words that drove each code (with translations). **This is the expensive artefact.**
5. `83-build-classification.py` — asserts every line's `review_id` against the index rebuilt from `reviews.jsonl`, refuses unknown codes, duplicate lines, unclassified reviews or hand-assigned mechanical codes, then emits `83-review-classification.py` (CLASSIFICATION, THEME_TO_IDS, LANG, META, NOTES). `META_LOW_INFO`, `META_EDITED`, `META_HAS_VOTES` and `META_NON_KOREAN` are assigned **mechanically**.
6. `83-validate.py` — **85 checks, all passing**: 10 structural (zero unknown IDs, zero intra-theme duplicates, zero unassigned records, every code used), 4 mechanical, 1 language, 38 forward text-to-code ties, 20 reverse vocabulary tests with every hit printed, 12 consistency rules. **The first run caught 27 real classification errors** (§9.C).
7. `83-aggregate.py` — every count, union, star band, storefront, eligible-country table, market group, language, era, month, paid segment, sensitivity run, vote and burst check, absence test and price point; writes `83-gen/`.
8. `83-build-report.py` assembles this report from hand-written sources and generated tables; `83-verify-report.py` re-derives its claims afterwards (§9.M).

## 1.5 Signal thresholds

Applied to the global corpus (denominator 500) and separately to KR (246), US (61) and JP (56) with their own denominators.

| Share | Label |
|---|---|
| <0.1% | Ignore by default |
| 0.1–<0.5% | Weak signal |
| 0.5–<1% | Emerging signal |
| 1–<3% | Meaningful signal |
| 3–5% | Very strong signal |
| >5% | High-priority signal |

**A caution on this scale at n = 500.** One review is 0.20% (Weak), three are 0.60% (Emerging), five are 1.00% (Meaningful) and 26 are 5.20% (High-priority). In the US a single review is 1.6% and in Japan 1.8%. Read the count and the confidence interval, not only the label, for anything under ten.

## 1.6 Limitations, stated plainly

1. **Positive selection** (warning 1). Unlike most app corpora, the reviewers mostly like the product; complaints are real but under-represented relative to praise.
2. **Three eligible countries.** Everything else is limited evidence.
3. **Bursts.** E4 is dominated by two bug waves; E4 shares describe those weeks.
4. **No version field; release notes only from 2025-09.** Eras are defined from the corpus.
5. **Purchases, prices and support contacts are self-reported.**
6. **The app was not tested**, and the letter and Dr. Eddy features could not be observed.
7. **Translations are the analyst's own.**
8. **Non-exclusive counts.** Codes overlap by design; unions give once-per-review counts.

---

# PART 2 — PRODUCT AND MONETIZATION MODEL

## 2.1 What the product is, from the reviews

A routine and to-do app built around a **baby bunny ("Roubit") that grows as you complete routines**. Completing routines earns **carrots**, then **coins**, **fragments** and **gems** spent on furniture, themes and outfits in the bunny's room, partly through a **gacha**. Around that loop: a **diary with mood logging**, an **AI "support letter" that replies to diary entries** (from April 2023), **Dr. Eddy**, an AI counsellor chat (reviews 2023-08 → 2024-10), **daily quotes and fortune cookies**, **statistics and weekly reports**, **alarms that repeat until done**, **NFC tag check-ins** (Korea), **widgets**, **invite codes**, and from January 2026 a **friend feature**.

| Feature | Evidence in reviews | Representative IDs |
|---|---|---|
| Bunny, room, carrots, gacha | `PRAISE_GAMIFICATION` 76; `REWARD_ECONOMY_SLOW` 7; `BUG_REWARDS` 15 | `8253963977`, `10531617064`, `12151980131`, `12518317305` |
| Support letter (AI reply to diary) | `PRAISE_SUPPORT_LETTER` 70; `PAYWALL_LETTERS_AI` 8; `BUG_LETTER_AI` 9 | `9866716493`, `11117435473`, `12298216994`, `12542235186` |
| Dr. Eddy (AI counsellor chat) | `PRAISE_DR_EDDY` 6; asked whether removed (`10597118888`, `14161345933`) | `10221201851`, `11714582957`, `11838028848` |
| Diary and mood log | `PRAISE_DIARY` 24; `BUG_DIARY_SETTINGS_BLANK` 40 | `9789513054`, `13303553647`, `13370425928` |
| Routines, to-dos, goals, D-day | `PRAISE_ALL_IN_ONE` 23; `BUG_ROUTINE_TRACKING` 20 | `11270169231`, `12713833538`, `12171575111` |
| Alarms and notifications | `PRAISE_REMINDERS` 9; `NOTIF_TOO_PERSISTENT` 3; `BUG_REMINDERS` 6 | `14080713944`, `12550130574`, `14357716177` |
| Quotes, fortune cookies, bunny speech | `PRAISE_QUOTES` 21 | `7844208245`, `10531617064` |
| Widgets | `PRAISE_WIDGET` 4; `BUG_WIDGET` 15; `REQ_WIDGET` 9 | `7793484497`, `11758713945`, `12898347981` |
| NFC tag check-in | `PRAISE_NFC` 2; stickers out of stock (`11213578473`) | `7685080703`, `8669867010` |
| Friends, invite codes | `SOCIAL_WITH_FRIENDS` 10; `REFERRAL_CODE` 3; `REQ_SOCIAL` 5 | `12223177608`, `9435490634` |
| Ads | `ADS_IN_APP` 12; `ADS_FOR_PAST_CHECK` 4 | `12509850218`, `13877341723` |

## 2.2 What the store listing says it is

Captured 13 September 2026 from the US and KR listings and 40 storefront lookups [external]:

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Name / bundle | Roubit : Fun Routine Planner ("Bunny Diary & Tasks") · `com.roubit`; KR: 루빗 - 귀여운 습관 할일 투두 계획 자기계발 루틴 |
| Seller | **Roubit Inc.** ((주)루빗) |
| Released / current | **2020-11-05** / **6.5.9**, 2026-09-08 |
| Price | **Free**, with in-app purchases; "Contains Advertising" |
| US subscriptions | Monthly Plan **$7.49**, Yearly Plan **$59.99** ("30%off first year" and "3months 30% off" variants), each "Unlimited Routines \| More Carrots \| No Ads", each with a Free Trial |
| KR subscriptions | 1달 **₩6,600**; 1년 **₩55,000**. Monthly: "무제한 반복 루틴 \| NFC 강제 태그 \| 더 많은 당근 \| 광고 제거". **Yearly adds "전용 테마·의상｜응원 메시지 무제한"** *[exclusive themes & outfits, unlimited support messages]* |
| Consumables | Gold Carrots: US $0.99 / $2.99 / $4.99 / $9.99; KR ₩1,100 / ₩4,400 / ₩7,700 / ₩17,000 |
| Languages | English and Korean |
| Age rating | 9+ (US) / 전체 (KR), with in-app controls, age assurance, "Health or Wellness Topics" |
| Public rating | US **4.72★ / 413** (352 / 34 / 11 / 4 / 12); KR **4.71★ / 1,492** (1,251 / 137 / 49 / 21 / 34); 40 storefronts **3,320** ratings, weighted **4.658★** |
| Privacy label | **Data Not Linked to You** only: usage, advertising data, diagnostics, precise location, email, user and device ID, purchase history. No "Data Used to Track You" section. |
| Support channels | cs@roubit.me; KR adds a Google Form and KakaoTalk 1:1 chat |

## 2.3 Free / paid / trial / unclear — classified

| Capability | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Download, routines up to a cap, diary, bunny, room | **Free** | `PRAISE_FREE_GENEROUS` 14; `11019712270` "無課金でも十分つかえる" *[usable enough without paying]* |
| More than 8 routines | **Paid.** Reviewers cite 8 (most), 9 (2023-02), 4 (2025-02), and a 150-record cap in 2021 | `FREE_LIMIT_ROUTINES` 12; listing "Unlimited Routines" |
| Support letters | **Limited free, unlimited on yearly (KR listing).** Reviewers cite 2 free replies, 2 a week, "second day you have to pay", ads to unlock | `PAYWALL_LETTERS_AI` 8; `11117435473`, `12290405993`, `12713450178`, `14077760217` |
| Dr. Eddy | **Unclear** — a 2026 reviewer asks whether it is gone or premium-only (`14161345933`) | `FEATURE_REMOVED` 6 |
| Ultimate themes and outfits | **Paid (yearly / "Ultimate").** Fragments cannot buy Ultimate items | `PAYWALL_COSMETICS` 4; `12165498115`, `12354142663` |
| No ads | **Paid** | listing; `12509850218` "the cost to remove the ads is about $60/yr" |
| Completing a past day's routine | **Ad-gated (free)** | `ADS_FOR_PAST_CHECK` 4 |
| NFC forced tag | **Monthly plan (KR listing); stickers sold separately** | listing; `10580947058` |
| Free trial | **7 days**, then billing | `TRIAL_USER` 4; `10783100144`, `12619312572` |
| Prices quoted by reviewers | ₩39,000 / year (2023-05), ₩38,000 discounted (2023-09), ₩55,000 / year (2024-10 onward), ₩4,400 (2025-05), $7.99 (2024-06), "about $60/yr" (2025-04), ¥6,000 / year (2026-03) | §9.G |

## 2.4 Data and privacy statements

| Source | What it says |
|---|---|
| App Store privacy label | **Data Not Linked to You** — including "Developer's Advertising or Marketing" and "Analytics" uses of usage and advertising data, and precise location for app functionality |
| KR description | optional permissions for files/media (routine memo, sharing) and camera (challenge verification) |

**One reviewer objects to tracking** — `13473580389` (#408, 2025-12-04, gb, 5★): "the app tracks you". No other review raises data handling. **The diary contains mental-health and crisis disclosures (§0.3) that are sent to an AI to generate letters**; no review asks where that text goes.

## 2.5 What could not be established

- **Downloads, conversion, revenue, refund and churn rates.**
- **Release history before 2025-09-16**, and therefore when the letter, Dr. Eddy, the gacha, fragments and the ad-gated past-day check were introduced, except as first dated in reviews.
- **What the AI letter does with crisis content**, and whether Dr. Eddy still exists.
- **Historical prices** — only reviewer quotes (§9.G) and the 2026 listing.
- **Why the diary tab blanked** in November 2025 and May 2026.


---

# PART 3 — GLOBAL FINDINGS

All denominators are 500 reviews, all 40 storefronts, 2020-11-14 → 2026-09-01, unless stated. Counts are non-exclusive; unions count each review once.

Six findings carry the corpus:

1. **Delight** — `U:praise_any` = 369 (73.80%): the bunny, the room loop and the letters (§3.2, §3.3).
2. **Emotional use** — `U:mental_health_any` = 68 (13.60%), including crisis disclosures (§3.4, §3.11).
3. **Reliability** — `U:defect_any` = 180 (36.00%) and `U:cant_use_app` = 88 (17.60%), concentrated in E4 (§3.5, §7).
4. **Light monetisation friction** — `U:paywall_friction` = 61 (12.20%), mostly caps, price and ads (§3.6).
5. **Requests from engaged users** — `U:request_any` = 86 (17.20%, mean 4.70★) (§3.9).
6. **Language** — `U:language_gap` = 16 (3.20%), concentrated in 2022–2023 (§3.8).

`U:negative_any` = 262 (52.40%) contain at least one negative code, at a mean of 3.50★.

- **Cute is the first word.** `PRAISE_CUTE` = 157 (31.40%, mean 4.50★). `12221992891` (#289, 2025-01-23, us, 5★) — "my bunny passed away recently and I kind of enjoy taking care of the rabbit in the app".
- **The loop motivates.** `PRAISE_GAMIFICATION` = 76 (15.20%, mean 4.68★). `7062422745` (#11, 2021-03-04, kr, 5★) — "햇빛을 받아서 새싹 키우는 재미까지" *[even the fun of growing a sprout in the sunlight]*.
- **All in one app.** `PRAISE_ALL_IN_ONE` = 23 (4.60%). `12713833538` (#346, kr) deleted separate to-do, goal and diary apps.
- **Design and inclusion.** `PRAISE_DESIGN_UI` = 43 (8.60%); `PRAISE_INCLUSIVE` = 2 (0.40%) — `7844208245` (#15, 2021-09-25, kr) — "성별 선택란에 여성 남성 이외에도 미정 란도 있어서 세심하다고 생각이 들었어요" *[the gender field has an 'undecided' option besides female and male; I found that thoughtful]*.
- **Free and ad-light.** `PRAISE_FREE_GENEROUS` = 14 (2.80%); `PRAISE_NO_ADS` = 6 (1.20%). `10444659530` (#135, 2023-10-06, kr) — "앞으로도 광고가 뜨지않는 이상은 꾸준히 사용할 것 같아요" *[as long as ads don't appear, I'll keep using it]*.
- **Generic praise.** `PRAISE_GENERIC` = 75 (15.00%) — "좋아요", "Love it"; 145 reviews overall are under 60 characters.

## 3.3 Outcomes, the letters and AI

| Code | Dir | n | % of 500 | CI 95% | Mean ★ | Signal | Review IDs |
|---|:-:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|---|
| `OUT_HABIT_BUILT` | + | 97 | 19.40% | 16.2–23.1% | 4.68 | High-priority signal | `6863577859`, `6872931078`, `6918523161`, `6959682378`, `7062422745`, `8255053505`, `8328675385`, `8453610137`, `8640048321`, `8827327386`, +87 more (§9.J) |
| `OUT_EMOTIONAL_SUPPORT` | + | 64 | 12.80% | 10.2–16.0% | 4.84 | High-priority signal | `8959038324`, `10365899636`, `10531617064`, `10783100144`, `10866655553`, `10868522829`, `11021196878`, `11042639191`, `11117435473`, `11145753252`, +54 more (§9.J) |

- **Habits.** `OUT_HABIT_BUILT` = 97 (19.40%, mean 4.68★). `9597261099` (#92, 2023-02-08, us) built "routines for home, health, self care, and work".
- **Comfort.** `OUT_EMOTIONAL_SUPPORT` = 64 (12.80%, mean 4.84★). `12390183328` (#318, 2025-03-06, au) — "I was having a lot of anxiety and stress" … "when I read the letter I felt those feelings slowly drift away".
- **The letter is known to be AI, and valued anyway.** `11180689493` (#193, 2024-04-20, us) — "Even if it's ai, they've always been really well done". `11353456949` (#206, 2024-06-07, kr) — "누군가는 인공지능의 답변이기에 무의미하다고 느낄 수도 있겠지만" *[some may find it meaningless because it's an AI's answer, but…]*.
- **Letters: limits, defects and dislike.** `PAYWALL_LETTERS_AI` = 8 (1.60%): `12542235186` (#331, 2025-04-14, jp, 2★) — "週に2回しかお返事が貰えないのが悲しいです" *[it's sad I only get replies twice a week]*; `10871827849` (#168, 2024-01-28, br, 2★) — "o diário tem que pagar pra receber a carta de IA, mas o app não avisou isso e do nada para de ter" *[you have to pay for the AI letter, but the app didn't warn you and it just stops]*. `BUG_LETTER_AI` = 9 (1.80%): `10594910174` (#144, 2023-11-17) — "챗GPT 편지를 안 물어보고 바로 넘어가요" *[it skips asking about the ChatGPT letter]*; `12348613423` (#312) — Dr. Eddy named the wrong author, "대표적으로 너의 이름은 이라고 알려줬어요" *[it told me the representative work was 'Your Name']*. `AI_CONTENT_DISLIKE` = 1 (0.20%): `13083050847` — "very cliche answers that don't resonate with me".
- **Dr. Eddy.** `PRAISE_DR_EDDY` = 6 (1.20%), all 2023-08-05 → 2024-10-22. `11838028848` (#245, ca, 4★) — "I especially love the Dr. Eddy feature because it's really useful and it helps me with my homework." `11714582957` (#232, jp) — "相談に乗ってくれるうさぎのエディー先生がいること" *[that there's Dr. Eddy, a rabbit who listens to your problems]*. Reviewers later ask if it was removed: `10597118888` (#145, 2023-11-18), `14161345933` (#489, 2026-06-09).

- **Mental health.** `USE_MENTAL_HEALTH` = 16 (3.20%, mean 4.75★) — ADHD (`12007216026`, `12151980131`, `12518317305`, `12713450178`), autism (`13303553647`), depression (`10866655553`, `11213578473`), anxiety (`11032700876`, `12390183328`), crisis (`12738770364`, `13873857942`).
- **Students and children.** `SEG_STUDENT_MINOR` = 13 (2.60%). `11366311382` (#207, gb) — "I am a high school student and it has helped me cope". `8775129558` (#60, 2022-06-15, kr, 5★) was blocked by sign-up age: "앱이4세 이상인데 14세이상 가입이니 조금 당황스러워요" *[the app is rated 4+ but sign-up is 14+; I'm a bit confused]*. `10308816687` (#129, kr) — "제가 초등학생이여서 이런걸 잘 모르는데" *[I'm in elementary school so I don't know about this]* (asking whether it keeps charging).
- **Switchers.** `SEG_TRIED_MANY` = 23 (4.60%, mean 4.91★) — among codes with ten or more reviews, only `REQ_CUSTOMISE_CHARACTER` (5.00★) has a higher mean.
- **Long-term users.** `SEG_LONG_TENURE` = 16 (3.20%, mean 3.56★); 11 of the 16 are in E4, most reporting the blank diary.
- **It is read as a game.** `META_CALLS_IT_GAME` = 6 (1.20%).

**`U:defect_any` = 180 (36.00%, mean 3.33★); `U:cant_use_app` = 88 (17.60%); `U:core_loop_defect` = 51 (10.20%).**

- **Blank Diary and Settings.** `BUG_DIARY_SETTINGS_BLANK` = 40 (8.00%) — §0.2 and §7.3. `14086081070` (#469, 2026-05-20, kr, 3★) — "업데이트할 때마다 버그가 새로 생기는 거 같아서 그냥 다른 어플로 갈까 생각중이에요" *[new bugs seem to appear with every update, so I'm thinking of switching apps]*.
- **Can't open or log in.** `BUG_LAUNCH_LOAD` = 26 (5.20%, mean 2.85★), `BUG_LOGIN_SIGNUP` = 16 (3.20%). An earlier cluster in March–April 2023: `9736739746` (#99) — "After 4 days the app just stopped working"; `9849991980` (#106, us, 2★) — "it breaks every like 3-4 days".
- **Forced update that cannot be installed.** `BUG_UPDATE_LOOP` = 7 (1.40%): once in 2022, then February–March 2025 and March–April 2026.
- **Core loop.** `BUG_ROUTINE_TRACKING` = 20 (4.00%), `BUG_REWARDS` = 15 (3.00%, mean 2.80★), `BUG_WIDGET` = 15 (3.00%), `BUG_REMINDERS` = 6 (1.20%). `11758713945` (#240, kr) — "제가 설정해둔 루틴의 순서가 위젯에서는 반대로 나오더라구요" *[my routine order shows reversed in the widget]*; the developer replied it would be fixed in October 2024 [external]. In July 2025 the widget disappeared: `12898347981` (#359, jp, 3★, a paying user) — "今回のアップデートでiPhoneのウィジェットが非対応になったようです" *[with this update the iPhone widget seems to be unsupported]*.
- **Lost items and diaries.** `DATA_LOSS` = 11 (2.20%, mean 2.91★). `10782496310` (#155, 2024-01-04, jp, 1★) — "アップデートしたら、日記が全部消えた。" *[after updating, all my diary entries disappeared]*. `14060686801` (#452, gb) — "It lost all my fragments. I had dozens."
- **Performance.** `BUG_PERFORMANCE` = 17 (3.40%): loading circles, lag, overheating.
- **Friction that is not a bug.** `UX_CLUNKY` = 18 (3.60%); `REDESIGN_DISLIKE` = 4 (0.80%); `FEATURE_BLOAT` = 3 (0.60%) — `10793137161` (#157) "방안 꾸미는 탭을 숨길 수 있는 기능이 있었으면 좋겠어요" *[I wish I could hide the room-decorating tab]*; `8411737665` (#44) left for a more minimal app, "토끼를 남겨두고 떠나기로 합니다" *[I've decided to leave the bunny behind]*; `ONBOARDING_LONG` = 1 (0.20%) — `11188145228` "延々と質問とプロモーションされ続けた" *[endless questions and promotions]*.

- **Routine cap.** `FREE_LIMIT_ROUTINES` = 12 (2.40%). `8981491200` (#71, 2022-08-15, kr, 1★) — "그냥 다른 앱 쓸게요" *[I'll just use another app]*. In 2021 the cap was on records: `6903629058` (#7) — "완전히 돈 내지 않으면 쓰지 마라 같네요" *[it's like 'don't use it unless you pay']*.
- **Price.** `PRICE_TOO_HIGH` = 10 (2.00%), eight of them in Korea. `9904411482` (#108, 2023-05-08, kr, 5★) — "39,000은 너무 비싼거같아요" *[₩39,000 seems too expensive]*. `12509850218` (#326, us, 4★) — "you have to watch an ad every try time you complete a task item" … "about $60/yr". `PRICE_FAIR` = 2: `11381876235` (#208) — "The price for premium honestly isn't that bad at 7.99".
- **Subscription format.** `WANT_ONE_TIME_PURCHASE` = 6 (1.20%). `9936992932` (#111, kr) — "구독제는 노예같은 느낌에" *[a subscription feels like slavery]*; `12555565779` (#334, jp) — "どうしても買い切りが欲しい" *[I really want a one-time purchase]*.
- **Price not visible.** `PRICE_OPAQUE` = 8 (1.60%). `12122537449` (#273, jp, 3★) — "わかりやすいところに価格を表示しないサービスはあまり信用ならないので" *[services that don't show the price clearly aren't very trustworthy]*.
- **Slow economy and paid cosmetics.** `REWARD_ECONOMY_SLOW` = 7 (1.40%): `12518317305` (#327) — "It is so hard to get the coins." `PAYWALL_COSMETICS` = 4 (0.80%): `12354142663` (#313, co, 2★) — "A bit greedy."
- **Ads.** §0.6.

## 3.8 Language and localisation

Codes `LANG_REQUEST` = 11 (2.20%) and `LOCALIZATION_INCOMPLETE` = 5 (1.00%) are in the defects table (§3.5). **`U:language_gap` = 16 (3.20%, mean 4.38★)** — reviewers who want to use the app, not reviewers leaving.

- **2022: Korean-only.** `8997324225` (#72, 2022-08-20, us, 5★) — "Will it be available in English?". The listing already claimed English (`9047952818`, §0.7).
- **2024–2026: other languages.** Chinese (`11397949222` "If you can support Chinese would be good"; `13458634572` "希望增加繁體中文！" *[please add Traditional Chinese]*), Vietnamese (`11042639191`).
- **Partial translation.** Korean in the gacha and store (`14090994337`, `14017493057`, `14113680514`); untranslated Japanese strings (`14273717431`); no am/pm time format (`10523193240`).
- **Korean reviews from outside Korea**: 6 (us 2, ca 2, jp 1, au 1) — Korean speakers abroad (§6.7).

`U:request_any` = 86 (17.20%, mean 4.70★) — **requests come from satisfied users**: 65 of them are five-star.

- **Platforms.** `REQ_IPAD_WATCH_MAC` = 15 (3.00%), all Korean. `8775133101` (#61, kr) — "프리미엄 구매하고싶은데 아이패드를 자주 쓰다보니 망설여져요" *[I want to buy premium but hesitate because I use my iPad a lot]*. `10332206320` (#131, kr, 3★, paying) uses Todo Mate instead: "가격 더 싸고 애플워치까지 호환성 좋은 투두메이트를 주로 쓰게 되는것 같아요" *[I end up mainly using Todo Mate, which is cheaper and works with Apple Watch]*.
- **Scheduling.** `REQ_FLEX_SCHEDULE` = 10 (2.00%): every N days, weekly counts, reps (`11780393009` "하루에 프랭크 10회 하기라면" *[if it's 10 planks a day]*), recurring deadlines and a pomodoro timer (`12518607166` "a pomodoro timer and the ability to set recurring deadlines"), a later day boundary (`9782795049` "자정을 넘기고 할 일을 하면 당근을 못 받는 게 의욕도 떨어지고" *[not getting carrots for tasks done after midnight kills motivation]*).
- **Character and room.** `REQ_CUSTOMISE_CHARACTER` = 14 (2.80%, mean 5.00★): outfits, poses, more rooms, seasonal themes.
- **Diary.** `REQ_DIARY_FEATURES` = 10 (2.00%): photos, more letters, more moods, memos. A paying user: `11116476663` (#184, jp) — "課金しているので、手紙が無制限なのはいいのですが、1日1通しか書けないのが困る" *[I pay so letters are unlimited, which is good, but I can only write one a day]*.
- **Widgets, social, sort order, font size.** `REQ_WIDGET` = 9; `REQ_SOCIAL` = 5 — `11031708917` "please add the ability to msg your friends and give them gifts"; a friend feature shipped in January 2026 [external]. `REQ_SORT_ORDER` = 5; `REQ_FONT_SIZE` = 3.

- **Questions.** `SUPPORT_QUESTION` = 43 (8.60%, mean 4.37★) — how to collect coins, join events, cancel, change language, fix errors.
- **Support failures.** `U:support_negative` = 15 (3.00%): `SUPPORT_UNRESPONSIVE` = 6, `SUPPORT_UNREACHABLE_INAPP` = 6, `NEGLECT_PERCEIVED` = 7 (1.40%) — `14156911446` (#488, 2026-06-08, kr, 2★) "앱이 뭔가 방치되는 느낌이 들기 시작하네요" *[it's starting to feel like the app is being neglected]*.
- **Churn.** `U:lost_user_signal` = 27 (5.40%, mean 2.33★): `CHURN_STATED` = 13, `CHURN_RISK` = 11, `ACCOUNT_DELETE` = 4. Stated reasons: ads (`13877341723`, `13956187658`, `14149256600`), bugs (`12958520430`, `14070811251`), the paywall (`8981491200`, `10948824494`, `10871827849`), minimalism (`8411737665`), a paid feature that never unlocked (`13852438902`).
- **Fraud language is almost absent.** `SCAM_LANGUAGE` = 2 (0.40%): `13852438902` (#431, jp, 1★) — "詐欺に近い対応です" *[it's close to fraud]*, and `12354142663` "A bit greedy."
- **Competitors named.** `COMP_NAMED_APP` = 8 (1.60%): Todo Mate, me+, Study Bunny, Finch, Duolingo (twice — as a nag to avoid, `13115443882` "it's not gonna harass you like Duolingo", and as a widget model, `13741115225`), Animal Crossing (as a price anchor), iPhone's built-in apps.

## 3.11 Privacy and safety

- **Privacy.** `PRIVACY_TRACKING` = 1 (0.20%, `13473580389`). No other data concern in 500 reviews (§9.F).
- **Age.** `AGE_GATE` = 1 (0.20%, `8775129558`); 13 reviews describe students or children (§3.4).
- **Crisis content.** Two reviews describe suicidal thoughts or a crisis (`12738770364`, `13873857942`) and 16 name a mental-health condition. For a feature that writes AI replies to diary entries, these are **Ignore-by-default counts that the thresholds exempt on safety grounds** (§1.5): the question is not frequency but handling.

**Praise falls and defects rise with every star lost, but defects are present at every level.** 93 of the 384 four- and five-star reviews (24.2%) report a defect; 46 of the 62 one- and two-star reviews (74.2%) do. Paywall friction peaks in the middle (20% of four- and three-star reviews, 29% of two-star). Requests are a five-star behaviour (22%); lost-user signals are a three-star-and-below behaviour.

## 4.3 Five-star reviews (301, 60.20%)

- **270 of 301 praise the app (90%)**; the letter (20%) and emotional support (19%) are more common here than in any other band.
- **94 of 301 are under 60 characters** ("좋아요", "Love it").
- **48 of 301 report a defect** and 25 could not use part of the app — five-star reviews are used to ask for fixes while signalling loyalty. `13370906696` (#391) and `14085465446` (#467) report the blank diary at five stars.
- **19 are complaint-only** (`META_RATING_TEXT_MISMATCH`, §4.7).

## 4.4 Four-star reviews (83, 16.60%)

*Love it, but…* — 67 of 83 praise the app (81%) and 45 report a defect (54%). The paywall is most visible here and at three stars (17 of 83, 20%): the routine cap (`10823956306`, `12214767986`), price (`12509850218`), letters (`12290405993`). `12007216026` (#260, ph) raised a 3★ to 4★ after reconsidering: "Quite literally the biggest incentive to getting premium is to support the devs".

## 4.5 Three-star reviews (54, 10.80%)

**Mostly broken, still liked.** 41 of 54 report a defect (76%) and 19 could not use part of the app (35%); 21 still praise it (39%). 10 of the 40 blank-diary reports are three-star. `12404186574` (#319, jp) — a white screen on launch, yesterday's routines shown, and an ad required to complete past tasks.

## 4.6 Two- and one-star reviews (62, 12.40%)

- **Two-star (17):** defects 11 (65%), paywall friction 5 (29%), `PAID_EXPLICIT` 3 (18%) — `10294277943`, `10996615256`, `14104835229`.
- **One-star (45):** defects 35 (78%), cannot use 20 (44%), launch failure 9 (20%), blank diary 9 (20%), lost-user signal 10 (22%).
- **16 of the 62 contain no defect code.** They are the routine cap (`8981491200`), ads (`9424779078`, `12354142663`, `13877341723`), price or one-time purchase (`10795669563`, `10965764229`), paid letters (`10871827849`, `12542235186`), a redesign (`11148269713`), onboarding (`11188145228`), account deletion (`10856644258`), cancel confusion (`12257979151`), an app that feels too much like a game to use (`11783666330`), a bare `11722165866` "I hate it", and two positive texts at one or two stars (`6918523161`, `13429711572`).

The other two are praise at one star (`6918523161`, "프리미엄 구독도 할 생각입니다" *[I'm planning to subscribe to premium]*; `13429711572`). **Star ratings in this corpus understate dissatisfaction during bug waves**; in E4, 19 of the 40 blank-screen reports are four- or five-star.

# PART 5 — PAID-USER AND UPGRADE ANALYSIS

## 5.1 The monetisation model

A **free core** (routines up to a cap, diary, bunny, room) with **ads** — including to complete past days and, from 2026, to earn extra rewards — and a **subscription**: monthly and yearly, with a 7-day free trial, removing ads and caps. The **yearly "Ultimate" plan** adds exclusive themes, outfits and unlimited support letters (KR listing). **Gold Carrots** are sold as consumables. Prices in 2026: US $7.49 / $59.99; KR ₩6,600 / ₩55,000 [external].

| Evidence | Finding |
|---|---|
| Reviewers who say they pay or paid | **`PAID_EXPLICIT` = 27 (5.40%)** — kr 17, jp 5, us 3, ph 1, sg 1 |
| …satisfied | **12 of 27 (44.4%)** |
| …report a defect | **14 of 27 (51.9%)** |
| …regret the purchase | 4 of 27 |
| …premium did not unlock or lapsed | 3 of 27, plus one trial user |
| …stated churn | 4 of 27 |
| Reviewers on a free trial | `TRIAL_USER` = 4 (0.80%) |
| Would pay, often with a condition | `WOULD_PAY_INTENT` = 12 (2.40%), 11 in Korea |

**No conversion, refund or retention rate can be derived from reviews.**

## 5.3 What buyers say they bought and why

| Trigger | Evidence |
|---|---|
| **More routines** | `PAID_FOR_ROUTINES` = 3 (0.60%). `9597261099` (#92, us) — "I have the paid version to add more routines in"; `12151980131` (#277, sg) — "the unlimited routines really make all the difference" |
| **The letters** | `PAID_FOR_LETTERS` = 3 (0.60%). `12298216994` (#300, kr) — "그래서 구독했습니다^^ 돈이 하나도 안아까울 정도로 만족하면서 사용하고 있어요" *[so I subscribed; I'm so satisfied the money isn't wasted at all]*; `11116476663` (#184, jp); `14104835229` (#475, jp) |
| **Falling for the bunny on day one** | `12713833538` (#346, kr) — "바로 프리미엄 결제했는데 절대 후회하지 않아여" *[paid for premium immediately and don't regret it at all]*; `9643626041` (#96, kr) paid "하루만에" *[after one day]*; `11254348101` (#201, kr) |
| **Supporting the developers** | `PAID_TO_SUPPORT_DEVS` = 1 — `12007216026` (#260, ph) |
| **NFC check-in** | `10580947058` (#143, kr) paid and bought stickers; the tag did not work |
| **Mental health** | 3 of 27 paying reviewers name a condition (`11213578473`, `12007216026`, `12151980131`) |

## 5.4 What goes wrong after purchase

- **Premium features not applied.** `PAID_NOT_UNLOCKED` = 4 (0.80%). `10623718154` (#147, us, 1★) — "I was stuck with only 8 even after paying" and support "never received any response back". `13852438902` (#431, jp, 1★) — "年額6000円のプランに課金したにも関わらず、有料機能が一切反映されず" *[despite paying for the ¥6,000 annual plan, none of the paid features applied]*; support promised a same-day fix, then three days of silence; refund requested. `10996615256` (#176, kr) — membership "가끔… 풀려서" *[sometimes drops]*. `13812322981` (#426, tr) — fixed by support via Restore Subscription.
- **The product broke under paying users.** 14 of 27 (51.9%) report a defect, including a reviewer who paid for the letters and then lost the diary: `14104835229` (#475, jp, 2★) — "しばらくして直ってるっぽかったらまた有料会員登録します" *[if it seems fixed after a while I'll subscribe again]*.
- **Regret.** `PAID_REGRET` = 4: `10294277943` (#128, kr) — too slow, no better than iPhone's built-in apps; `10800299917` (#159, us) — abandoned a year's subscription as clunky.

## 5.5 Upgrade barriers among non-buyers

| Barrier | n | % of 500 | Representative |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Price too high (`PRICE_TOO_HIGH`) | 10 | 2.00% | `9904411482`, `11959502740`, `12509850218` |
| Wants a one-time purchase (`WANT_ONE_TIME_PURCHASE`) | 6 | 1.20% | `10795669563`, `12555565779` |
| Price not visible / recurring? (`PRICE_OPAQUE`) | 8 | 1.60% | `12122537449`, `12677103918`, `10308816687` |
| Needs iPad or Watch first | — | — | `8775133101`, `10332206320` |
| Wants bugs fixed first | — | — | `11208683126` "빠른 개선 이루어지면 유료 전환 후 길게 쓸 의향 있음" *[if bugs are fixed quickly, I'll switch to paid and use it long-term]* |
| A "free" trial that charged | 2 | 0.40% | `12619312572`, `11872679326` |

**The barrier is subscription format and price relative to a to-do app, not the value of the character or the letters.** Korean reviewers anchor on the yearly price (₩39,000 in 2023, ₩55,000 from 2024) and on one-time purchases; two quote a discount (`10332206320` ₩38,000) and one waits for a sale (`11959502740`). The listing ran a 40% New Year Ultimate discount in January 2026 [external].

## 5.6 What cannot be claimed here

- **No conversion rate.** 27 paying reviewers against 12 would-be buyers is not a ratio of anything.
- **No price-elasticity finding.** Price objections are 10 reviews, eight in Korea.
- **No finding that ads drive upgrades.** No reviewer says they paid to remove ads.

## 6.2 Eligibility

The threshold is 50 reviews. **South Korea (KR 246), the United States (US 61) and Japan (JP 56) qualify**; together they are 363 of 500 (72.60%). The next largest are Brazil 24, the UK 13 and Mexico 13; 17 storefronts have exactly one review. All 500 reviews are in every global figure.

Signal labels use the Korean denominator (246); one review is 0.4%. Korean reviews span 2020–2026 (2021: 22, 2022: 54, 2023: 46, 2024: 44, 2025: 50, 2026: 29).

**What is distinctive about Korea (with counts):**

- **Engaged requesters.** `U:request_any` 55 of 246 (22.4%) vs 12.2% elsewhere; **`REQ_IPAD_WATCH_MAC` 15 of 246 (6.1%) — every such request in the corpus**; `REQ_CUSTOMISE_CHARACTER` 11 of 246 (4.5%) vs 1.2%.
- **The buyers and would-be buyers.** `PAID_EXPLICIT` 17 of 246 (6.9%) vs 3.9%; `WOULD_PAY_INTENT` 11 of 246 (4.5%) vs 0.4%; `PRICE_TOO_HIGH` 8 of 246 (3.3%) vs 0.8%; `WANT_ONE_TIME_PURCHASE` 5 of 246 (2.0%) vs 0.4%.
- **Widgets matter.** `BUG_WIDGET` 12 of 246 (4.9%) vs 1.2%.
- **Short and support-like.** `META_LOW_INFO` 87 of 246 (35.4%) vs 22.8%; `SUPPORT_QUESTION` 27 of 246 (11.0%) vs 6.3%; `META_RATING_TEXT_MISMATCH` 23 of 246 (9.3%) vs 3.9%.
- **Fewer letter and outcome mentions.** `PRAISE_SUPPORT_LETTER` 25 of 246 (10.2%) vs 17.7%; `OUT_EMOTIONAL_SUPPORT` 23 of 246 (9.3%) vs 16.1%; `PRAISE_FREE_GENEROUS` 1 of 246.
- **Public rating 4.71★ over 1,492 ratings; 1★ is 34 of 1,492 (2.28%) publicly and 14 of 246 (5.69%) in text.**

- **No platform requests** (0 of 61 for iPad or Watch).
- **Public rating 4.72★ over 413 ratings.**

Signal labels use the Japanese denominator (56); one review is 1.8%. **Japanese reviews are recent**: one in 2022, then 12 in 2024, 22 in 2025 and 21 in 2026.

**What is distinctive about Japan (with counts):**

- **The blank-diary market.** `BUG_DIARY_SETTINGS_BLANK` 18 of 56 (32.1%) vs 5.0% elsewhere; `U:defect_any` 34 of 56 (60.7%) vs 32.9%; `U:cant_use_app` 21 of 56 (37.5%). Eight of the 14 first-wave reports (November 2025) are Japanese.
- **Support and churn.** `U:support_negative` 7 of 56 (12.5%) vs 1.8%; `U:lost_user_signal` 8 of 56 (14.3%) vs 4.3%; `DATA_LOSS` 3 of 56 (5.4%).
- **Ads.** `U:ads_any` 4 of 56 (7.1%) vs 2.7% — `13877341723`, `13956187658`, `12404186574`, `13202890811`.
- **Visible to other shoppers.** `META_HAS_VOTES` 17 of 56 (30.4%) vs 3.2% — 17 of the 31 voted reviews in the corpus are Japanese, and 10 of them are blank-diary or bug reports.
- **Still valued.** `PRAISE_GAMIFICATION` 11 of 56 (19.6%); `PRAISE_SUPPORT_LETTER` 9 of 56 (16.1%).
- **Public rating 4.48★ over 576 ratings — the largest corpus–public gap among eligible storefronts (+1.13★).**

## 6.6 High-spend markets and high-review-volume markets (both defined)

**High-spend group — defined, not measured.** No spend or download data exists. For comparability with the other reports in this series the same list is used: **us, gb, au, ca, de, ch, fr, it, es, nl, be, at, no, fi, sg, ae**. Eleven are present (ch, it, be, at, no are not). **The US is 61 of the group's 103 reviews.** Korea and Japan are not in the list; both are large App Store markets, so this group understates the high-spend reviewers in this corpus.

**High-review-volume group** — storefronts with ≥10 reviews: **kr, us, jp, br, gb, mx**. A review-volume proxy, not a download ranking.

**Reading:**

- **Outside Korea, reliability is the bigger issue.** High-volume storefronts without Korea: `U:cant_use_app` 38 of 167 (22.8%) against 17.6% overall; mean 3.904★.
- **The defined high-spend group is more positive and less broken** — defects 28.2% vs 38.0%, letter praise 20.4% vs 13.4% — but it is mostly the US and excludes Korea and Japan.
- **Requests are a high-volume behaviour** (18.9% vs 9.2%), driven by Korea.

## 6.8 Corpus versus public ratings

**Corpus 4.156★ vs 4.658★ across 3,320 public ratings (+0.502★).** The gap is small where reviews are mostly praise and large where they are mostly bug reports: KR +0.34★, US +0.52★, BR +0.69★, **JP +1.13★**. Unlike apps where the public rating is suspect, here **the text reviews are the more negative signal, and they lead it**: Japan's text reviews turned during the bug waves, while its cumulative public rating still reads 4.48★.

# PART 7 — TIME-TREND ANALYSIS

## 7.1 Method

Reviews have no `version` field and the listing shows release notes only from 2025-09-16 (§9.E). Eras are defined by **what the corpus itself dates**:

- **E1 — Korean-only early years** (2020-11-14 → 2022-07-31): every review is in Korean.
- **E2 — English-speaking discovery, before AI letters** (2022-08-01 → 2023-04-26): starts with the first English-language request (2022-08-20 is the first `LANG_REQUEST`; the era boundary is the start of that month).
- **E3 — AI letters and global growth** (2023-04-27 → 2025-11-05): starts the day the support letter first appears in a review.
- **E4 — Diary/Settings blank waves, ads and Ultimate** (2025-11-06 → 2026-09-01): starts with the first blank-diary report.

| Era | Label | Window | Days | n | Reviews / 30 days | Mean ★ | 5/4/3/2/1★ | 1–2★ share | Non-Korean | Under 60 chars | With votes |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| **E1** | Korean-only early years | 2020-11-14 → 2022-07-31 | 625 | 68 | 3.26 | 4.69 | 55/9/2/0/2 | 2.9% | 0 (0.0%) | 23 (33.8%) | 5 (7.4%) |
| **E2** | English-speaking discovery, before AI letters | 2022-08-01 → 2023-04-26 | 269 | 38 | 4.24 | 4.24 | 24/6/4/1/3 | 10.5% | 15 (39.5%) | 15 (39.5%) | 1 (2.6%) |
| **E3** | AI letters and global growth | 2023-04-27 → 2025-11-05 | 924 | 277 | 8.99 | 4.28 | 173/52/25/11/16 | 9.7% | 152 (54.9%) | 69 (24.9%) | 13 (4.7%) |
| **E4** | Diary/Settings blank waves, ads and Ultimate | 2025-11-06 → 2026-09-01 | 300 | 117 | 11.70 | 3.52 | 49/16/23/5/24 | 24.8% | 80 (68.4%) | 38 (32.5%) | 12 (10.3%) |

## 7.3 The two blank-screen waves

| Wave | Window | Reviews in window | Blank Diary/Settings | Storefronts (blank) |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| **1** | 2025-11-06 → 2025-11-10 | 15 | 14 | KR 6, JP 8 |
| **2** | 2026-05-16 → 2026-06-09 | 37 | 26 | KR 11, JP 10, GR, IN, TW, MX, VN 1 each |

- **Wave 1** is 14 of the 15 reviews posted over five days: eight on 2025-11-08 alone, the busiest day in the corpus. Its release context is the 2025-11-05 version 6.1.24 ("Dreaming Idol" theme; "fixed… login errors and issues with deleting groups") and 6.1.27 on 2025-11-11 [external]. **Timing, not proven cause.**
- **Wave 2** is 26 of the 37 reviews posted 2026-05-16 → 2026-06-09, and 36 of the 37 carry a defect code. Its release context is 6.4.50 (2026-05-15, iOS subscription fixes, "A new option to watch ads and earn additional rewards") and 6.4.52 (2026-06-09, "If the diary list cannot be loaded, a helpful message is shown instead of a blank screen") [external]. `14138599777` (#480, 2026-06-03, jp) says it is the second time: "今回で2回目です" *[this is the second time]*. `14153188568` (#486, kr) says the problem has recurred for six months: "6개월 전부터 일기, 설정 을 들어거면 흰 화면만뜨고" *[for six months, opening Diary or Settings shows only a white screen]*.
- **After 2026-06-09**, 11 reviews mention no blank Diary tab. With so few reviews, **the absence is weak evidence of a fix**; the app's own later notes still address diary saving (6.5.6, 2026-08-14).

## 7.4 What rose and what fell

- **The letter carried E3.** `PRAISE_SUPPORT_LETTER` first appears on 2023-04-27; in E3 it is 60 of 277 (21.7%). `PAYWALL_LETTERS_AI` is first on 2024-01-10, when a reviewer found letters stopping after the first (`10804179194`).
- **English-speaking discovery came first, localisation later.** `LANG_REQUEST` is first on 2022-08-20; 7 of 38 (18.4%) E2 reviews. By E3 non-Korean reviews are 152 of 277 (54.9%), and in E4 80 of 117 (68.4%).
- **Defects rose before the waves.** E3 `U:defect_any` 76 of 277 (27.4%), against 10 of 68 (14.7%) in E1.
- **Requests declined as the app broke.** `U:request_any` 36.8% in E2 → 7.7% in E4.
- **Paywall complaints are concentrated in E3**: `FREE_LIMIT_ROUTINES` 8, `PRICE_TOO_HIGH` 9, `WANT_ONE_TIME_PURCHASE` 6, `PAYWALL_LETTERS_AI` 7 — none of the last three in E4.
- **E3 mean 4.28★ vs E4 mean 3.52★**; one- and two-star share 9.7% → 24.8%.

## 7.5 Early signals already present before E4

- A March–April 2023 cluster of "an error has occurred" launch failures (`9736739746`, `9741384614`, `9765970115`, `9849991980`).
- Update-loop reports in February 2025 (`12335505100`, `12335524722`, `12342994567`) and March 2025 (`12371418626`).
- Widget removal in July 2025 (`12896744449`, `12898347981`) and perceived neglect from August 2025 (`12990089428`).

---

# PART 8 — PRODUCT IMPLICATIONS

Every recommendation is tied to findings by section number and labelled **Immediate fix**, **Experiment** or **Research question**. For a team building in this category, the same items read as **what to copy and what to avoid**.

## 8.1 Immediate fix — release safety for the diary, and a way out of Settings (§0.2, §3.5, §7.3)

**Evidence:** `BUG_DIARY_SETTINGS_BLANK` = 40 (8.00%) in two waves; 18 of 56 (32.1%) Japanese reviews; `DATA_LOSS` = 11 (2.20%); `SUPPORT_UNREACHABLE_INAPP` = 6 (1.20%); 19 of the 40 blank-screen reports at four or five stars; a reviewer reporting a second occurrence (`14138599777`).

**Recommendation:** treat the diary as irreplaceable personal data — a release gate on Diary and Settings opening on current iOS versions, staged rollout with crash and blank-screen telemetry, and a fallback read-only diary view. **Move the support form and account deletion somewhere that does not depend on Settings loading.** Offer diary export.

**Why first:** it breaks the feature people pay for (letters) and the feature that holds their history, and it takes the reporting route with it.

## 8.2 Immediate fix — first launch and login (§0.4, §6.4)

**Evidence:** `BUG_LAUNCH_LOAD` = 26 (5.20%); US 7 of 61 (11.5%); `BUG_LOGIN_SIGNUP` = 16 (3.20%) including Google and Facebook login and lost items (`13156744094`, `14113680514`); `BUG_UPDATE_LOOP` = 7 (1.40%).

**Recommendation:** instrument the path from install to the home screen (room loading, routine fetch, forced-update check) and fail open: show routines when the room or store cannot load. Never block the app on an update that the App Store does not yet offer.

## 8.3 Immediate fix — a safety protocol for the letter and Dr. Eddy (§0.3, §3.3, §3.11)

**Evidence:** `U:mental_health_any` = 68 (13.60%); two crisis disclosures (`12738770364`, `13873857942`); a reviewer, `13429711572`, who valued the letter for "sempre indicando a procura por um profissional"; Dr. Eddy answering factual questions wrongly (`12348613423`) and used for homework (`11838028848`).

**Recommendation:** document and test how the AI responds to self-harm, crisis and minor users; always surface crisis resources when triggered; label the letter as AI. **Research question:** do minors reach the letter despite the 14+ sign-up gate (`8775129558`, `10308816687`)?

## 8.4 Immediate fix — keep ads out of the habit record (§0.6, §3.6)

**Evidence:** `ADS_FOR_PAST_CHECK` = 4 (0.80%) since 2022; `ADS_IN_APP` = 12 (2.40%); churn from ads (`13877341723`, `13956187658`, `14149256600`); an ad that failed to load blocked completion for a month (`13956187658`).

**Recommendation:** let yesterday's routine be completed without an ad; if an ad cannot load, complete anyway. Keep rewards, not records, ad-gated.

## 8.5 Experiment — pricing format in Korea and Japan (§0.5, §5.5)

**Evidence:** `WANT_ONE_TIME_PURCHASE` = 6 (1.20%); `PRICE_TOO_HIGH` = 10 (2.00%), 8 in Korea; `WOULD_PAY_INTENT` 11 of 246 Korean reviews; `PRICE_OPAQUE` = 8 (1.60%); a student segment (`SEG_STUDENT_MINOR` = 13).

**Experiment:** test a lifetime or long-term plan and a student price against the yearly ₩55,000 plan, and show price and renewal terms before the trial. Measure paid conversion and letter usage, not only revenue per user.

## 8.6 Experiment — what the free letter allowance should be (§3.3, §2.3)

**Evidence:** `U:letter_ai_praise` = 74 (14.80%); `PAYWALL_LETTERS_AI` = 8 (1.60%) with reviewers unsure of the rule ("I'm not sure though", `11117435473`) or surprised when letters stopped (`10871827849`); letters named as a purchase reason (`PAID_FOR_LETTERS` = 3).

**Experiment:** state the allowance before the user writes; compare "2 per week" against "1 per day" on retention and subscription. The letter is the product's differentiator; the paywall should not make it a surprise.

## 8.7 Immediate fix — complete the localisation (§0.7, §3.8)

**Evidence:** `LOCALIZATION_INCOMPLETE` = 5 (1.00%) in 2023–2026 (Korean in the gacha and store); `LANG_REQUEST` = 11 (2.20%); 247 non-Korean reviews (49.40%).

**Recommendation:** string coverage checks for every release in each shipped language; decide on Chinese and Vietnamese from install data.

## 8.8 Experiment — the requests satisfied users make (§3.9)

**Evidence:** `REQ_IPAD_WATCH_MAC` = 15 (3.00%, Korea only, one blocking a purchase); `REQ_FLEX_SCHEDULE` = 10 (2.00%); `REQ_CUSTOMISE_CHARACTER` = 14 (2.80%); widget reliability (`BUG_WIDGET` = 15, 3.00%).

**Experiment:** an iPad layout for Korea; flexible recurrence (every N days, counts); widget check-off. Sequence after §8.1–§8.2.

## 8.9 Research questions

- **What caused the two blank-screen waves**, and why was Japan over-represented?
- **What does the AI letter do with crisis and minor content?**
- **Did the 2026 ad changes reduce retention among long-term free users?**
- **How many paying users are affected when premium does not apply** (`PAID_NOT_UNLOCKED`), and how fast does support resolve it?

## 8.10 What this corpus says to copy — and not to copy

| Copy | Why |
|---|---|
| A character whose room grows with completed routines | `U:character_game` = 192 (38.40%); "the only one that survived" (§0.1) |
| An empathetic reply to journaling | `U:letter_ai_praise` = 74 (14.80%); emotional outcomes (§3.3) |
| A usable free tier | `PRAISE_FREE_GENEROUS` = 14 (2.80%) against 12 routine-cap complaints (§3.6) |

| Don't | Why |
|---|---|
| Ship without guarding the diary and Settings | 40 blank-screen reports and lost support access (§0.2) |
| Put ads between the user and their habit record | churn from ads in 2026 (§0.6) |
| Leave AI responses to crisis content untested | two crisis disclosures (§0.3) |
| Read cumulative 4.66★ as current satisfaction | Japan's text reviews average 3.36★ (§6.8) |

---

# PART 9 — EVIDENCE APPENDIX

## 9.A Counting rules

1. **Denominator** is 500 (every review in `reviews.jsonl`) unless the sentence names a narrower one: a star band, an era, a storefront (KR 246, US 61, JP 56), the rest of the corpus, a market group, a language, or a paid segment. Segment rates are written "n of N".
2. **Codes are non-exclusive** (mean 4.01 per review including mechanical codes). **Unions count each review once.**
3. **Mean ★** for a code is the mean rating of the reviews carrying it.
4. **Signal band** is applied to the share of the stated denominator (§1.5); Wilson 95% intervals throughout.
5. **Mechanical codes** — `META_LOW_INFO` (HTML-unescaped, stripped body under 60 characters), `META_EDITED` (`is_edited` true), `META_HAS_VOTES` (`vote_count` > 0), `META_NON_KOREAN` (hand-assigned language not Korean and not emoji-only) — are assigned by the build, never by hand.
6. **`PAID_EXPLICIT`** requires the reviewer to say they subscribe, subscribed, bought premium, pay, or had a subscription to cancel. A free trial alone is `TRIAL_USER`; an unexpected charge alone is `BILL_UNEXPECTED_CHARGE`.
7. **`BUG_DIARY_SETTINGS_BLANK`** requires the Diary or Settings tab to be named with a white screen, freeze or no response. A white screen before the home screen loads is `BUG_LAUNCH_LOAD`.
8. **`PRAISE_SUPPORT_LETTER`** includes replies to the diary described as advice, letters or AI; Dr. Eddy is coded only when named or described as a chat.
9. **`PRAISE_GENERIC`** is assigned when a review approves but names no quality; it never co-occurs with specific praise or `PRAISE_BEST` (check C1).
10. **`META_RATING_TEXT_MISMATCH`** is four or five stars with no praise code and at least one negative code, or one or two stars with praise and no negative code (checks C6–C7); neutral questions and requests are exempt (§9.C).
11. **Eras** are E1 2020-11-14 → 2022-07-31, E2 2022-08-01 → 2023-04-26, E3 2023-04-27 → 2025-11-05, E4 2025-11-06 → 2026-09-01.
12. **Language** is hand-assigned and checked against Hangul, kana, Han and Cyrillic script detection and an English function-word test (check L1).
13. **No deduplication** was performed; none was needed (§1.3).

## 9.B Taxonomy — all 112 codes

| Code | Dir | Family | n | Definition |
|---|:-:|---|---:|---|
| `PRAISE_ALL_IN_ONE` | + | Praise | 23 | Values routines + to-dos + diary + goals/D-day together in one app. |
| `PRAISE_BEST` | + | Praise | 37 | Best / favourite routine app, or 'settled on this one'. |
| `PRAISE_CUTE` | + | Praise | 157 | Bunny/character, art, colours or overall look called cute, pretty, adorable, kawaii, aesthetic. |
| `PRAISE_DESIGN_UI` | + | Praise | 43 | Clean, simple, intuitive, easy-to-use or well-crafted interface/layout. |
| `PRAISE_DEV_RESPONSIVE` | + | Praise | 4 | Developers listen, update, run surveys, communicate or fixed a problem. |
| `PRAISE_DIARY` | + | Praise | 24 | Values the diary/journal or mood log itself (beyond the letter). |
| `PRAISE_DR_EDDY` | + | Praise | 6 | Values Dr. Eddy / the bunny counsellor chat (AI advice). |
| `PRAISE_FREE_GENEROUS` | + | Praise | 14 | Says the free version is genuinely usable / features are not locked behind a paywall. |
| `PRAISE_GAMIFICATION` | + | Praise | 76 | Carrots/coins/rewards, room decorating, growing the bunny or plant, levels, missions, gacha motivate the reviewer. |
| `PRAISE_GENERIC` | + | Praise | 75 | Approves but names no product quality ('good', 'love it', 'thanks'). |
| `PRAISE_INCLUSIVE` | + | Praise | 2 | Values inclusive or respectful sign-up details: non-binary gender option, minimal consents. |
| `PRAISE_NFC` | + | Praise | 2 | Values the NFC tag/sticker check-in feature. |
| `PRAISE_NO_ADS` | + | Praise | 6 | Says there are no or few ads, or that ads were removed. |
| `PRAISE_QUOTES` | + | Praise | 21 | Values daily quotes, fortune cookies, positive messages or the bunny's speech bubbles. |
| `PRAISE_RECOMMENDED_ROUTINES` | + | Praise | 6 | Values suggested/recommended routines, to-dos or goals. |
| `PRAISE_REMINDERS` | + | Praise | 9 | Values reminders/alarms, including persistent notifications until done. |
| `PRAISE_STATS` | + | Praise | 12 | Values statistics, reports, achievement rates or monthly views. |
| `PRAISE_SUPPORT_LETTER` | + | Praise | 70 | Values the (AI) support letter / reply to a diary entry. |
| `PRAISE_WIDGET` | + | Praise | 4 | Values a home-screen widget. |
| `OUT_EMOTIONAL_SUPPORT` | + | Outcome | 64 | Says the app comforted them or helped mood, stress, anxiety, depression, loneliness or mental health. |
| `OUT_HABIT_BUILT` | + | Outcome | 97 | Says the app helped build habits, routines, consistency, motivation or productivity. |
| `ACQ_SOCIAL` | o | Use | 5 | Found the app via social media (Twitter, Instagram, SNS), an ad, a friend or family. |
| `META_CALLS_IT_GAME` | o | Use | 6 | Calls the app a game ('this game', 'healing game', 'players'). |
| `SEG_LONG_TENURE` | o | Use | 16 | States use of about six months or more (e.g. '1 year', '2 years', 'level 70'). |
| `SEG_RETURNING` | o | Use | 6 | Came back to the app after using it before. |
| `SEG_STUDENT_MINOR` | o | Use | 13 | Says they are a student, child or minor, or installed it for one. |
| `SEG_TRIED_MANY` | o | Use | 23 | Says they tried many/other routine apps before this one. |
| `SOCIAL_WITH_FRIENDS` | o | Use | 10 | Uses the app with friends/family (visits, invites, shared graphs, gifts). |
| `USE_MENTAL_HEALTH` | o | Use | 16 | Names a mental-health or neurodivergent context: depression, anxiety, ADHD, autism, suicidal thoughts, crisis. |
| `USE_OTHER` | o | Use | 9 | Names another concrete use: parenting, diabetes, work, school, chores, early rising, phone detox. |
| `PAID_EXPLICIT` | o | Paid users | 27 | Says they subscribed, bought premium/Ultimate, started a paid or card-charged plan, or are a paying user. |
| `PAID_FOR_LETTERS` | o | Paid users | 3 | Names support letters / AI replies as the reason they paid or would pay. |
| `PAID_FOR_ROUTINES` | o | Paid users | 3 | Names more/unlimited routines as the reason they paid or would pay. |
| `PAID_NOT_UNLOCKED` | - | Paid users | 4 | Paid, but premium features did not apply, lapsed, or ads still showed. |
| `PAID_REGRET` | - | Paid users | 4 | A paying reviewer regrets the purchase / says it is not worth the money. |
| `PAID_SATISFIED` | + | Paid users | 12 | A paying reviewer is satisfied / says it is worth the money. |
| `PAID_TO_SUPPORT_DEVS` | o | Paid users | 1 | Paid mainly to support the developers. |
| `TRIAL_USER` | o | Paid users | 4 | Says they are on (or started) a free trial of premium. |
| `WOULD_PAY_INTENT` | ~ | Paid users | 12 | Intends or would like to pay (possibly conditional on a fix, price or platform). |
| `BILL_REFUND_REQUEST` | o | Billing | 2 | Asks for a refund or says they requested one. |
| `BILL_UNEXPECTED_CHARGE` | - | Billing | 2 | Charged unexpectedly: a 'free trial' charged, or a sudden annual renewal. |
| `CANCEL_HOWTO` | o | Billing | 3 | Asks how to cancel a subscription/trial or whether it will be cancelled. |

## 9.C Validation, and what it caught

`83-validate.py` runs **85 checks, all passing** (output in `Temp/83-validate-out.txt`; log in `Temp/83-validation-log.md`): 10 structural, 4 mechanical, 1 language, 38 forward text-to-code ties, 20 reverse vocabulary tests with every hit printed and read, and 12 consistency rules.

**The first run failed 16 of 85 checks. 27 hand-file lines were real classification errors, fixed:**

| Check | Reviews | Error | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | #8, #58, #170, #199, #252, #321 | generic praise co-coded with specific praise | `PRAISE_GENERIC` removed |
| C6 | #191, #408 | mismatch flag on a consistent 1★ complaint, and on a mixed 5★ | flag removed |
| C7 | #23, #40, #74, #78, #298, #299, #310, #311, #341, #343, #344, #375, #417, #457, #461, #467, #489 | approving text ("잘 쓰고 있는데", "I love the app but", "Amo esta aplicación") with no praise code | `PRAISE_GENERIC` added |
| C7 | #7, #148 | complaint-only 4–5★ not flagged | `META_RATING_TEXT_MISMATCH` added |

**The other failures were defects in the checks**, each fixed: missing vocabulary ("돈이 아깝", "정액", "돈내고", "비싼", "support", "respostas"); a cancel pattern that matched "해지" inside "편해지고"; expected-code lists that lacked free-tier praise; and an unused code (`META_NO_CONTENT`, removed).

**Documented exemptions:** L1 — `13618102099` (Spanish in Unicode math letters), `12412492785` (Turkish with a Korean translation); R3 — `6903629058`, `10800299917` (false hit "adds to"), `12162728348`; R11 — `12007216026` (negated); R12 — `11255613213` (troubleshooting); R18 — seven reviews where "년" is a price, plan length, age wait or depression duration; C7 — `9322787093`, `10433314206`, `10580947058`, `12301064819`, `13868299608` (neutral requests or questions).

**Known residual error risk.** Letter vs Dr. Eddy is ambiguous where reviewers say only "advice" (`10165948188`, `11867791246`). `PAID_EXPLICIT` includes a reviewer who forgot to cancel a subscription that may have been a trial (`12873882242`). Mild-praise boundaries are judgement calls; checks enforce consistency, not correctness.

## 9.D Sensitivity

| Measure | Full (500) | Without reviews under 60 chars (355) | Without Korea (254) | Without E4 (to 2025-11-05) (383) | Without rating–text mismatches (467) |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Mean rating | 4.156 | 4.152 | 3.945 | 4.350 | 4.139 |
| `U:praise_any` | 73.8% | 79.7% | 74.4% | 80.2% | 78.6% |
| `U:praise_specific` | 56.8% | 67.6% | 59.8% | 62.1% | 60.4% |
| `U:letter_ai_praise` | 14.8% | 19.2% | 18.5% | 16.7% | 15.6% |
| `PRAISE_GAMIFICATION` | 15.2% | 20.8% | 15.4% | 17.0% | 16.3% |
| `OUT_EMOTIONAL_SUPPORT` | 12.8% | 15.2% | 16.1% | 13.6% | 13.5% |
| `U:defect_any` | 36.0% | 37.7% | 38.6% | 24.5% | 32.8% |
| `U:cant_use_app` | 17.6% | 17.7% | 21.3% | 8.6% | 14.8% |
| `BUG_DIARY_SETTINGS_BLANK` | 8.0% | 8.2% | 9.1% | 0.0% | 7.1% |
| `U:paywall_friction` | 12.2% | 14.6% | 12.6% | 14.1% | 12.6% |
| `PAID_EXPLICIT` | 5.4% | 7.0% | 3.9% | 6.3% | 5.8% |
| `U:language_gap` | 3.2% | 3.1% | 5.9% | 2.6% | 3.4% |
| `PRAISE_GENERIC` | 15.0% | 11.5% | 14.2% | 16.2% | 16.1% |

**Dropping the 145 short reviews raises specific praise** (56.8% → 67.6%) and letter praise (14.8% → 19.2%). **Dropping Korea** lowers the mean to 3.945★ and raises `U:cant_use_app` to 21.3%. **Dropping E4** removes the blank-diary finding entirely and lowers defects to 24.5% — the reliability finding is an E4 finding. **Dropping the mismatches** lowers defects to 32.8%. No headline in Part 0 other than the E4-specific ones changes direction.

Full notes: `Temp/83-external.md`, `Temp/83-listing-extract.txt`. **Not available:** downloads, revenue, conversion or refund rates; release notes before 2025-09-16; historical prices; the Wayback Machine was not queried.

## 9.H Burst, solicitation and authenticity checks

- **No exact-duplicate text, no repeat authors, no duplicate IDs** (§1.3).
- **Busiest days are bug days.** 8 reviews on 2025-11-08, all eight reporting the blank diary (KR 3, JP 5, mean 3.50★); 4 each on 2026-05-17 and 2026-05-18. **An event response, not a positive burst.** The only busy positive day is 2022-01-18 (4 reviews, mean 5.00★), when two reviewers mention the developer's survey (`8253845821`, `8254693656`).
- **Prompted reviews.** `REVIEW_INCENTIVE` = 4 (0.80%), all January–March 2022: `8483099861` (#51) — "이번에도 어플관련 설문지 응답하면서 하는 김에 리뷰도 쓰러 왔어요" *[answering the app survey again, I came to write a review while I was at it]*; `8462169519` (#50) — "업뎃 됬는데 리뷰쓰기 미션이 인증이 안되네요" *[after the update, the review-writing mission isn't verified]* — **an in-app mission for writing a review**. Rewarding reviews would lift the public rating regardless of experience; four reviews cannot show its scale.
- **Invite codes.** `REFERRAL_CODE` = 3 (0.60%), 2022-12 → 2024-04: `9435490634` (#86) — "초대코드 입력하시고 도시 테마 받아가세요" *[enter the invite code and get the city theme]*.
- **Reviews removed?** `8101412932` (#21, 2021-12-06) titled "왜 리뷰가 없어질까요" *[why do reviews disappear]*. Unverifiable.
- **Helpful votes are rare and negative-leaning.** 31 reviews carry votes (max 5); mean votes per review by band: 0.07 / 0.08 / 0.07 / 0.12 / 0.22 (5★ → 1★). 17 of the 31 are Japanese.
- **Edited:** 22. **Rating–text contradictions:** 33 (§4.7).

**Reading.** Beyond four prompted reviews and three invite codes in 2021–2024, the corpus shows no pattern consistent with coordinated positive reviews. The public rating's weakness is that it is cumulative, not that it looks manipulated (§6.8).

## 9.I Developer and support responses

**On the listing** [external]: six developer responses are visible — three US (2023-02-14, 2024-04-05, 2024-08-19; thank-you templates) and three KR (2021-12-12; 2024-05-28 on NFC stickers and overheating; 2024-09-26 promising the widget-order fix "10월 중에" *[in October]*).

**Support contacts described in reviews:**

| Review | What happened (as reported) |
|---|---|
| `13303553647` (#383) | bug "sorted within 24 hours"; "customer service is absolutely amazing" |
| `13812322981` (#426) | support fixed trial ads via Restore Subscription; rating raised to 5★ |
| `8462169519` (#50) | inquiry handled by SMS for a reviewer without KakaoTalk |
| `13852438902` (#431) | told "iOSの互換性の問題が生じており、本日中に解決する" *[an iOS compatibility issue, resolved today]*; then three days of silence |
| `14138599777` (#480) | two emails, no reply |
| `14142547272` (#481) | KakaoTalk inquiry "읽지도 않아요" *[not even read]* |
| `13956187658` (#443) | reported the ad problem; no improvement for a month |
| `10623718154` (#147) | "never received any response back" |
